+(* SCOPE NOTE: standalone proof scope.
+   Generic all-action refinement theorem over a supplied relation.
+   It proves safety for that interface, not unconditional native-machine
+   enforcement. ConcreteClosureBridge instantiates it for the original host. *)
+
+(* Simulation of selected guest programs and enforcement of every host action
+   have different quantifiers. This is the sufficient all-host-action contract. *)
+From Coq Require Import List.
+Import ListNotations.
+
+Section Refinement.
+Context {Guest Host GuestMove HostMove : Type}.
+Variable guest_step : Guest -> GuestMove -> Guest.
+Variable host_step : Host -> HostMove -> Host.
+Fixpoint guest_run (w : list GuestMove) (s : Guest) :=
+  match w with [] => s | i::rest => guest_run rest (guest_step s i) end.
+Fixpoint host_run (w : list HostMove) (s : Host) :=
+  match w with [] => s | i::rest => host_run rest (host_step s i) end.
+
+Variable related : Guest -> Host -> Prop.
+Hypothesis every_host_step_refines : forall g h,
+  related g h -> forall i,
+  exists w, related (guest_run w g) (host_step h i).
+
+Lemma guest_run_append : forall u v g,
+  guest_run (u++v) g = guest_run v (guest_run u g).
+Proof. induction u; intros; cbn; [reflexivity|apply IHu]. Qed.
+
+Theorem every_host_trace_refines : forall w g h,
+  related g h -> exists v, related (guest_run v g) (host_run w h).
+Proof.
+  induction w as [|i w IH]; intros g h H; cbn.
+  - exists []; exact H.
+  - destruct (every_host_step_refines g h H i) as [chunk Hchunk].
+    destruct (IH _ _ Hchunk) as [tail Htail].
+    exists (chunk++tail); rewrite guest_run_append; exact Htail.
+Qed.
+
+Variable guest_invariant : Guest -> Prop.
+Variable host_invariant : Host -> Prop.
+Hypothesis guest_preserves : forall g i,
+  guest_invariant g -> guest_invariant (guest_step g i).
+Hypothesis relation_transfers_invariant : forall g h,
+  related g h -> guest_invariant g -> host_invariant h.
+
+Lemma guest_trace_preserves : forall w g,
+  guest_invariant g -> guest_invariant (guest_run w g).
+Proof. induction w; intros; cbn; [assumption|apply IHw, guest_preserves; assumption]. Qed.
+
+Theorem all_host_programs_preserve : forall w g h,
+  related g h -> guest_invariant g -> host_invariant (host_run w h).
+Proof.
+  intros w g h Hr Hi.
+  destruct (every_host_trace_refines w g h Hr) as [v Hv].
+  eapply relation_transfers_invariant; [exact Hv|apply guest_trace_preserves; exact Hi].
+Qed.
+
+Variable guest_accepts : Guest -> bool.
+Variable host_accepts : Host -> bool.
+Hypothesis acceptance_reflects : forall g h,
+  related g h -> host_accepts h = true -> guest_accepts g = true.
+
+(* Evidence is a predicate on a GUEST trace: its earned chain, for example.
+   Producing evidence about the HOST trace itself requires a further link. *)
+Theorem accepted_host_run_has_guest_evidence : forall g h
+    (evidence : list GuestMove -> Prop),
+  related g h ->
+  (forall v, guest_accepts (guest_run v g) = true -> evidence v) ->
+  forall w, host_accepts (host_run w h) = true ->
+  exists v, related (guest_run v g) (host_run w h) /\ evidence v.
+Proof.
+  intros g h evidence Hr He w Ha.
+  destruct (every_host_trace_refines w g h Hr) as [v Hv].
+  exists v; split; [exact Hv|apply He; eapply acceptance_reflects; eassumption].
+Qed.
+End Refinement.
+
+Print Assumptions every_host_trace_refines.
+Print Assumptions all_host_programs_preserve.
+Print Assumptions accepted_host_run_has_guest_evidence.

+From Coq Require Import List.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Minimal.EarnedCore Minimal.UniversalThiele
+  Kernel.UniversalCertificationCost Kernel.UniversalThieleLinks.
+Module E := Minimal.EarnedCore.
+Module U := Minimal.UniversalThiele.

+(* The combined reading describes own OR guest establishment. It is not the
+   guest mirror and must not be substituted for it in provenance claims. *)
+Theorem combined_reading_is_not_guest_acceptance :
+  let h := U.hrun (map U.OWN
+    [E.CHECK E.PZero E.CA; E.COMMIT E.PZero E.CA; E.CERTIFY])
+      (U.hload 0 0 [] 0 0) in
+  U.hread h = true /\ U.mrec h = false /\ E.cert (U.gst h) = false.
+Proof. repeat split; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+(* An arbitrary state constructor is not an authorized clean start. *)
+Theorem provenance_needs_a_start_condition :
+  let s := E.mkst (E.start_core 0 0) 0 true in
+  E.cert (E.run [] s) = true /\ E.total_cost [] = 0 /\ ~ E.clean_start s.
+Proof.
+  cbn; split; [reflexivity|split; [reflexivity|]].
+  unfold E.clean_start; cbn; intros [_ [_ H]]; discriminate.
+Qed.

+From Coq Require Import Arith Lia.
+Require Minimal.ThieleComplete Minimal.NeutralComplete Minimal.NeutralMore
+  Minimal.SlicedEstablishment Audit.StructuralBridge.
+Module T := Minimal.ThieleComplete.
+Module N := Minimal.NeutralComplete.
+Module X := Minimal.NeutralMore.
+Module S := Minimal.SlicedEstablishment.
+
+Section FixedRule.
+Variable State : Type.
+Variable next : State -> State.
+Variable K : Type.
+Variable event : K -> State -> bool.
+
+Definition fixed_rule_base : T.machine :=
+  T.mk_machine State unit (fun s _ => next s) (fun _ => 0) (fun _ => false).
+
+Definition fixed_rule_lift_is_sliced :
+  S.SlicedInterface (N.nstate fixed_rule_base) State unit K :=
+  StructuralBridge.lift_as_sliced fixed_rule_base K event.
+
+Theorem fixed_rule_lift_is_not_neutral_complete :
+  ~ N.neutral_complete (N.nlift fixed_rule_base K event).
+Proof.
+  apply X.nlift_finitely_branching_not_nc.
+  intro s; exists 1, (fun _ => 0); split.
+  - intros; lia.
+  - intros; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+(* These two interfaces are not interchangeable. This does not say that
+   the base cannot compute, or that no adaptive interpreter can run on it. *)
+Theorem sliced_does_not_require_this_completeness :
+  inhabited (S.SlicedInterface (N.nstate fixed_rule_base) State unit K) /\
+  ~ N.neutral_complete (N.nlift fixed_rule_base K event).
+Proof.
+  split; [constructor; exact fixed_rule_lift_is_sliced|
+    exact fixed_rule_lift_is_not_neutral_complete].
+Qed.
+End FixedRule.
+
+Print Assumptions fixed_rule_lift_is_sliced.
+Print Assumptions fixed_rule_lift_is_not_neutral_complete.
+Print Assumptions sliced_does_not_require_this_completeness.

+(* A countermodel to reading macro-endpoint simulation as prefix safety.
+   Both machines meet the actual abstract CertificationSystem definition. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Bool Arith Lia.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Kernel.UniversalCertificationCost.
+Module C := Kernel.UniversalCertificationCost.
+
+Inductive host_move := Raise | Reset.
+Definition hstep (_ : bool) (i : host_move) :=
+  match i with Raise => true | Reset => false end.
+Definition hprice (i : host_move) := match i with Raise => 1 | Reset => 0 end.
+
+Definition host : C.CertificationSystem.
+Proof.
+  refine (C.mk_cert_system bool host_move hstep hprice (fun b => b) _).
+  intros s [|] H0 H1; cbn in *; [lia|discriminate].
+Defined.
+Definition guest : C.CertificationSystem.
+Proof.
+  refine (C.mk_cert_system unit unit (fun s _ => s) (fun _ => 0) (fun _ => false) _).
+  intros; discriminate.
+Defined.
+Definition simulation : C.SimulatingCertificationSystem host.
+Proof.
+  refine (@C.Build_SimulatingCertificationSystem host guest
+    (fun _ => [Raise;Reset]) (fun _ => false) _ _); intros; reflexivity.
+Defined.
+
+Theorem every_macro_boundary_matches : forall w,
+  C.cs_run host (concat (map (C.scs_decode simulation) w)) false = false.
+Proof.
+  intro w; change (C.cs_run host (concat (map (C.scs_decode simulation) w))
+    (C.scs_embed simulation tt) = false).
+  rewrite <- C.scs_run_embed; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem macro_prefix_exposes_unmatched_acceptance :
+  C.cs_cert guest tt = false /\
+  C.cs_cert host (C.cs_run host [Raise] false) = true /\
+  C.cs_cert host (C.cs_run host [Raise;Reset] false) = false /\
+  C.cs_total_cost host [Raise] = 1.
+Proof. repeat split; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+(* The false-to-true-to-false counterexample relies on the abstract model
+   not requiring permanence. It cannot be used against a permanent host. *)
+Theorem this_host_is_not_permanent :
+  ~ (forall s i, C.cs_cert host s = true -> C.cs_cert host (C.cs_step host s i) = true).
+Proof. intro H; specialize (H true Reset eq_refl); discriminate. Qed.
+
+Section PermanentBoundary.
+Variable machine : C.CertificationSystem.
+Hypothesis permanent : forall s i,
+  C.cs_cert machine s = true -> C.cs_cert machine (C.cs_step machine s i) = true.
+Lemma permanent_trace : forall w s,
+  C.cs_cert machine s = true -> C.cs_cert machine (C.cs_run machine w s) = true.
+Proof. induction w; intros; cbn; [assumption|apply IHw, permanent; assumption]. Qed.
+
+Theorem false_endpoint_excludes_accepting_prefix : forall prefix suffix s,
+  C.cs_cert machine (C.cs_run machine (prefix++suffix) s) = false ->
+  C.cs_cert machine (C.cs_run machine prefix s) = false.
+Proof.
+  intros prefix suffix s H; rewrite C.cs_run_app in H.
+  destruct (C.cs_cert machine (C.cs_run machine prefix s)) eqn:Hp; [|reflexivity].
+  pose proof (permanent_trace suffix _ Hp); congruence.
+Qed.
+End PermanentBoundary.
+
+Print Assumptions simulation.
+Print Assumptions every_macro_boundary_matches.
+Print Assumptions macro_prefix_exposes_unmatched_acceptance.
+Print Assumptions this_host_is_not_permanent.
+Print Assumptions false_endpoint_excludes_accepting_prefix.

+(* Neutral completeness does not force mark silence for its chosen window. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Bool Arith Lia.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Minimal.ThieleComplete Minimal.NeutralComplete.
+Module T := Minimal.ThieleComplete.
+Module N := Minimal.NeutralComplete.
+
+Inductive move := Compute (i : T.cm_instr) | Mark.
+Definition state := (T.cm_conf * bool * nat)%type.
+Definition step (s : state) (i : move) : state :=
+  let '(x,r,l) := s in
+  match i with
+  | Compute b => (T.cm_exec b x,r,l)
+  | Mark => (T.cm_exec (T.CINC T.RA) x, r || Nat.eqb (fst (snd x)) 0,S l)
+  end.
+Definition price (i : move) := match i with Compute _ => 0 | Mark => 1 end.
+Definition machine := T.mk_machine state move step price (fun s => snd (fst s)).
+Definition window (s : state) := fst (fst s).
+Definition load (a b : nat) : state := ((1,(a,b)),false,0).
+
+Definition base : T.universal_base machine.
+Proof.
+  refine (T.mk_ub machine window (fun _ => True) Compute load _ _ _).
+  - reflexivity.
+  - intros; exact I.
+  - intros [[x r] l] i _; split; [reflexivity|exact I].
+Defined.
+
+Definition interface : N.neutral_interface machine :=
+  N.mk_ni machine base (fun i => match i with Compute _ => true | Mark => false end)
+    (fun s => snd s).
+
+Theorem complete_with_changing_mark : N.neutral_complete_with interface.
+Proof.
+  split.
+  - split; [reflexivity|]. split.
+    + intros [[x r] l] [i|] H; [reflexivity|discriminate].
+    + intros [[x r] l] [i|] Hr; cbn in *; [exact Hr|rewrite Hr; reflexivity].
+  - split; [intros a b; reflexivity|]. split.
+    + split; [intros [i|]; reflexivity|]. intros [[x r] l] [i|]; cbn; lia.
+    + exists [Mark],0,0,1,0; split; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem chosen_window_is_not_mark_silent :
+  window (step (load 0 0) Mark) <> window (load 0 0).
+Proof. discriminate. Qed.
+
+(* The claim is about this window, not every possible projection. *)
+Theorem neutral_interface_does_not_imply_vertical_silence :
+  N.neutral_complete_with interface /\
+  ~ (forall s, window (step s Mark) = window s).
+Proof.
+  split; [apply complete_with_changing_mark|].
+  intro H; apply chosen_window_is_not_mark_silent; apply H.
+Qed.
+
+Print Assumptions complete_with_changing_mark.
+Print Assumptions chosen_window_is_not_mark_silent.
+Print Assumptions neutral_interface_does_not_imply_vertical_silence.

+(* SCOPE NOTE: standalone proof scope.
+   Generic output/transition factorization with an explicit section.
+   It tests the proposed quotient interpretation without assuming a product
+   decomposition or the native certification implementation. *)
+
+(* Exact conditions for implementing an observation on the chosen base.
+   A supplied section avoids any quotient or choice axiom. *)
+From Coq Require Import List.
+Import ListNotations.
+
+Section Contract.
+Context {State Base Input Answer : Type}.
+Variable view : State -> Base.
+Variable representative : Base -> State.
+Hypothesis section_law : forall b, view (representative b) = b.
+Variable step : Input -> State -> State.
+Variable answer : State -> Answer.
+
+Definition output_factors := exists decode : Base -> Answer,
+  forall s, decode (view s) = answer s.
+Definition output_constant := forall s t,
+  view s = view t -> answer s = answer t.
+Definition step_factors := exists shadow : Input -> Base -> Base,
+  forall i s, view (step i s) = shadow i (view s).
+Definition step_stable := forall i s t,
+  view s = view t -> view (step i s) = view (step i t).
+
+Theorem output_factorization_iff : output_factors <-> output_constant.
+Proof.
+  split.
+  - intros [decode H] s t E; rewrite <- (H s), <- (H t), E; reflexivity.
+  - intro H; exists (fun b => answer (representative b)); intro s.
+    apply H; apply section_law.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem transition_factorization_iff : step_factors <-> step_stable.
+Proof.
+  split.
+  - intros [shadow H] i s t E; rewrite !H, E; reflexivity.
+  - intro H; exists (fun i b => view (step i (representative b))).
+    intros i s; apply H; symmetry; apply section_law.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem base_only_implementation_iff :
+  (output_factors /\ step_factors) <-> (output_constant /\ step_stable).
+Proof. rewrite output_factorization_iff, transition_factorization_iff; reflexivity. Qed.
+End Contract.
+
+Section ChangeOfEncoding.
+Context {State Host Input Answer Base : Type}.
+Variable step : Input -> State -> State.
+Variable host_step : Input -> Host -> Host.
+Variable encode : State -> Host.
+Variable answer : State -> Answer.
+Variable host_answer : Host -> Answer.
+Hypothesis commutes : forall i s, encode (step i s) = host_step i (encode s).
+Hypothesis preserves_answer : forall s, host_answer (encode s) = answer s.
+
+Fixpoint run (w : list Input) (s : State) :=
+  match w with [] => s | i::rest => run rest (step i s) end.
+Fixpoint host_run (w : list Input) (s : Host) :=
+  match w with [] => s | i::rest => host_run rest (host_step i s) end.
+
+Theorem encoding_preserves_runs : forall w s,
+  encode (run w s) = host_run w (encode s).
+Proof. induction w; intro s; cbn; [reflexivity|rewrite IHw, commutes; reflexivity]. Qed.
+
+Theorem encoding_cannot_merge_distinguishable_states : forall s t w,
+  answer (run w s) <> answer (run w t) -> encode s <> encode t.
+Proof.
+  intros s t w H E; apply H.
+  rewrite <- !preserves_answer, !encoding_preserves_runs, E; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+Variable view : State -> Base.
+Variable host_view : Host -> Base.
+Hypothesis preserves_view : forall s, host_view (encode s) = view s.
+
+Theorem hidden_pair_survives_view_preserving_encoding : forall s t,
+  view s = view t -> answer s <> answer t ->
+  encode s <> encode t /\ host_view (encode s) = host_view (encode t) /\
+  host_answer (encode s) <> host_answer (encode t).
+Proof.
+  intros s t Hv Ha; split.
+  - intro E; apply Ha; rewrite <- !preserves_answer, E; reflexivity.
+  - split; [rewrite !preserves_view|rewrite !preserves_answer]; assumption.
+Qed.
+End ChangeOfEncoding.
+
+Print Assumptions output_factorization_iff.
+Print Assumptions transition_factorization_iff.
+Print Assumptions base_only_implementation_iff.
+Print Assumptions encoding_preserves_runs.
+Print Assumptions encoding_cannot_merge_distinguishable_states.
+Print Assumptions hidden_pair_survives_view_preserving_encoding.

+(* A mathematical extension used ONLY as a countermodel. ForceMirror is NOT
+   an instruction of the repository's original universal host. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Bool Arith Lia.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Minimal.EarnedCore Minimal.UniversalThiele
+  Kernel.UniversalCertificationCost Kernel.UniversalThieleLinks.
+Module E := Minimal.EarnedCore.
+Module U := Minimal.UniversalThiele.
+Module C := Kernel.UniversalCertificationCost.
+Module L := Kernel.UniversalThieleLinks.
+
+Inductive extended_move := Existing (i : U.hinstr) | ForceMirror.
+Definition extended_step (h : U.hstate) (i : extended_move) :=
+  match i with
+  | Existing j => U.hexec h j
+  | ForceMirror => U.mkh (U.hcore h) (U.hmu h + 1) (U.hcert h)
+      (U.gprog h) (U.gst h) true
+  end.
+Definition extended_cost (i : extended_move) :=
+  match i with Existing j => U.hcost j | ForceMirror => 1 end.
+
+Definition extended_system : C.CertificationSystem.
+Proof.
+  refine (C.mk_cert_system U.hstate extended_move extended_step extended_cost U.mrec _).
+  intros h [i|] H0 H1.
+  - exact (U.host_mirror_toll h i H0 H1).
+  - cbn; lia.
+Defined.
+
+Definition original_host_is_simulated : C.SimulatingCertificationSystem extended_system.
+Proof.
+  refine (@C.Build_SimulatingCertificationSystem extended_system L.host_mirror_cs
+    (fun i => [Existing i]) (fun h => h) _ _); intros; reflexivity.
+Defined.
+
+Theorem original_traces_are_preserved : forall tr h,
+  C.cs_run extended_system (map Existing tr) h = U.hrun tr h.
+Proof. induction tr; intro h; cbn; [reflexivity|apply IHtr]. Qed.
+
+Theorem original_costs_are_preserved : forall tr,
+  C.cs_total_cost extended_system (map Existing tr) = U.htotal_cost tr.
+Proof. induction tr; cbn; [reflexivity|rewrite IHtr; reflexivity]. Qed.
+
+Theorem extension_has_exact_ledger : forall h i,
+  U.hmu (extended_step h i) = U.hmu h + extended_cost i.
+Proof. intros h [i|]; [apply U.hmu_conservation|reflexivity]. Qed.
+
+Theorem extension_has_permanent_record : forall h i,
+  U.mrec h = true -> U.mrec (extended_step h i) = true.
+Proof.
+  intros h [[j|b]|] Hr; cbn; try exact Hr; try reflexivity.
+  destruct (E.err (U.hcore h)); cbn; [exact Hr|rewrite Hr; reflexivity].
+Qed.
+
+Definition loaded_empty := U.hload 0 0 [] 0 0.
+Definition bypassed := extended_step loaded_empty ForceMirror.
+
+Theorem paid_raise_without_guest_establishment :
+  U.mrec bypassed = true /\ E.cert (U.gst bypassed) = false /\
+  E.clean_start (U.gst bypassed) /\ U.hmu bypassed = 1 /\
+  extended_cost ForceMirror = 1.
+Proof. repeat split; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem empty_guest_cannot_supply_accepting_execution : forall n,
+  E.cert (E.run_prog n [] (E.start 0 0)) = false.
+Proof. intro n; rewrite E.run_prog_halted; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem no_original_host_trace_reaches_bypassed : forall tr,
+  U.hrun tr loaded_empty <> bypassed.
+Proof.
+  intros tr H.
+  pose proof (U.record_agreement tr loaded_empty (U.hload_agrees 0 0 [] 0 0)) as Ha.
+  rewrite H in Ha; unfold U.agrees in Ha; discriminate.
+Qed.
+
+Print Assumptions extended_system.
+Print Assumptions original_host_is_simulated.
+Print Assumptions original_traces_are_preserved.
+Print Assumptions original_costs_are_preserved.
+Print Assumptions extension_has_exact_ledger.
+Print Assumptions extension_has_permanent_record.
+Print Assumptions paid_raise_without_guest_establishment.
+Print Assumptions empty_guest_cannot_supply_accepting_execution.
+Print Assumptions no_original_host_trace_reaches_bypassed.

+(* SCOPE NOTE: standalone proof scope.
+   Standalone three-state counterexample to deriving an entry toll
+   from source-dependent collision coverage. It deliberately does not satisfy
+   the stronger Thiele entry-toll axiom and is not a native-machine instance. *)
+
+(* A state-dependent collision charge need not charge the entry transition. *)
+From Coq Require Import Arith Lia.
+Inductive state := unfinished | first_held | final_held.
+Definition step (s : state) :=
+  match s with unfinished => first_held | _ => final_held end.
+Definition reading (s : state) :=
+  match s with unfinished => false | _ => true end.
+Definition charge (s : state) :=
+  match s with first_held => 1 | _ => 0 end.
+Definition ledger (s : state) :=
+  match s with final_held => 1 | _ => 0 end.
+
+Theorem permanence : forall s, reading s = true -> reading (step s) = true.
+Proof. destruct s; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem exact_accounting : forall s, ledger (step s) = ledger s + charge s.
+Proof. destruct s; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem every_collision_has_paid_source : forall x y,
+  x <> y -> step x = step y -> 1 <= charge x + charge y.
+Proof. destruct x,y; cbn; intros; try congruence; lia. Qed.
+
+Theorem entry_is_free : reading unfinished = false /\
+  reading (step unfinished) = true /\ charge unfinished = 0.
+Proof. repeat split; reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem entry_has_no_competing_source : forall s,
+  step s = step unfinished -> s = unfinished.
+Proof. destruct s; cbn; intros; congruence. Qed.
+
+Print Assumptions permanence.
+Print Assumptions exact_accounting.
+Print Assumptions every_collision_has_paid_source.
+Print Assumptions entry_is_free.
+Print Assumptions entry_has_no_competing_source.

+(* SCOPE NOTE: standalone proof scope.
+   Generic transport of Minimal.Representation capacity through
+   a supplied exact encoding. Independence and exact recovery are hypotheses;
+   no claim that every native run realizes arbitrary profiles is assumed. *)
+
+(* Transport the existing capacity bound through an arbitrary exact encoding. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Bool Arith Lia.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Minimal.Representation.
+Module R := Minimal.Representation.
+
+Section Capacity.
+Context {State Host Base : Type}.
+Variable view : State -> Base.
+Variable profile : State -> list bool.
+Variable realize : list bool -> State.
+Variable encode : State -> Host.
+Variable host_view : Host -> Base.
+Variable host_decode : Host -> list bool.
+Hypothesis preserves_view : forall s, host_view (encode s) = view s.
+Hypothesis preserves_profile : forall s, host_decode (encode s) = profile s.
+
+Theorem faithful_encoding_preserves_fibre_bound : forall m b fibre,
+  (forall bits, length bits = m -> view (realize bits) = b /\
+    profile (realize bits) = bits) ->
+  (forall h, host_view h = b -> In h fibre) ->
+  2^m <= length fibre.
+Proof.
+  intros m b fibre Hreal Hcover.
+  apply (R.independent_profiles_capacity host_view host_decode
+    (fun bits => encode (realize bits)) b m fibre).
+  - intros bits Hlen; rewrite preserves_view, preserves_profile; apply Hreal; exact Hlen.
+  - exact Hcover.
+Qed.
+End Capacity.
+
+(* The capacity obligation alone needs no ledger, versions or certification:
+   an ordinary list of m bits attains the exact finite bound. *)
+Section Attained.
+Context {Base : Type}.
+Variable b : Base.
+Definition bit_states m := map (fun bits => (b,bits)) (R.profiles m).
+
+Theorem ordinary_bits_attain_the_bound : forall m,
+  NoDup (bit_states m) /\ length (bit_states m) = 2^m /\
+  (forall bits, length bits = m ->
+    In (b,bits) (bit_states m) /\ fst (b,bits) = b /\ snd (b,bits) = bits).
+Proof.
+  intro m; split.
+  - apply R.map_injective_nodup; [apply R.profiles_nodup|intros; congruence].
+  - split.
+    + unfold bit_states; rewrite map_length, R.profiles_length; reflexivity.
+    + intros bits H; split.
+      * apply in_map; apply R.profiles_complete; exact H.
+      * split; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+End Attained.
+
+(* One Boolean question needs only two states, however long the history.
+   Requiring one latch is not requiring recovery of every individual event. *)
+Definition any_record (history : list bool) := existsb (fun b => b) history.
+Theorem one_latch_does_not_require_history_recovery :
+  any_record [true;false] = any_record [false;true] /\
+  [true;false] <> [false;true].
+Proof. split; [reflexivity|discriminate]. Qed.
+
+Print Assumptions faithful_encoding_preserves_fibre_bound.
+Print Assumptions ordinary_bits_attain_the_bound.
+Print Assumptions one_latch_does_not_require_history_recovery.

+(* Structural lift bridge. No universality, effectivity, or old-cost preservation
+   is assumed. The named counterexamples in Audit pin the limits of the claim. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Bool Arith Lia.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Minimal.ThieleComplete Minimal.NeutralComplete Minimal.SlicedEstablishment.
+Module T := Minimal.ThieleComplete.
+Module N := Minimal.NeutralComplete.
+Module S := Minimal.SlicedEstablishment.
+
+Section Bridge.
+Variable M : T.machine.
+Variable K : Type.
+Variable event : K -> T.m_state M -> bool.
+
+Definition base_view (s : N.nstate M) := fst (fst s).
+Definition record_view (s : N.nstate M) := snd (fst s).
+Definition embed (x : T.m_state M) : N.nstate M := (x,false,0).
+
+(* No universal_base hypothesis: the very same neutral lift has this interface
+   over ANY deterministic base, including a single fixed next-state rule. *)
+Definition lift_as_sliced : S.SlicedInterface (N.nstate M) (T.m_state M)
+    (T.m_move M) K.
+Proof.
+  refine {| S.view := base_view; S.verdict := record_view;
+    S.horizontal := fun i s => N.nstep M K event s (N.NBase i);
+    S.vertical := fun k s => N.nstep M K event s (N.NMark k);
+    S.shadow_step := fun i x => T.m_step M x i;
+    S.vertical_price := fun _ => 1 |}.
+  - intro x; exists (embed x); reflexivity.
+  - intros i [[x r] l]; reflexivity.
+  - intros i [[x r] l]; reflexivity.
+  - intros k [[x r] l]; reflexivity.
+  - intros k [[x r] l] H; cbn in *; rewrite H; reflexivity.
+  - intros; lia.
+Defined.
+
+Theorem projection_is_retraction : forall x, base_view (embed x) = x.
+Proof. reflexivity. Qed.
+
+Theorem full_horizontal_embedding : forall w x,
+  T.run (N.nlift M K event) (map (@N.NBase M K) w) (embed x)
+    = embed (T.run M w x).
+Proof.
+  induction w as [|i w IH]; intro x; [reflexivity|].
+  change (T.run (N.nlift M K event) (map (@N.NBase M K) w)
+    (embed (T.m_step M x i)) = embed (T.run M w (T.m_step M x i))).
+  apply IH.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem mixed_projection_erases_only_marks : forall w x r l,
+  base_view (T.run (N.nlift M K event) w (x,r,l)) =
+    T.run M (N.nbases M K w) x.
+Proof. intros; apply N.nlift_run_base. Qed.
+
+Theorem exact_ledger : forall s i,
+  snd (N.nstep M K event s i) = snd s + N.ncost M K i.
+Proof. intros [[x r] l] [i|k]; cbn; lia. Qed.
+
+Theorem raised_record_cannot_be_decoded_from_base : forall k x,
+  event k x = true ->
+  ~ exists decode : T.m_state M -> bool,
+      forall s, decode (base_view s) = record_view s.
+Proof.
+  intros k x He [decode H].
+  pose proof (H (embed x)) as H0.
+  pose proof (H (N.nstep M K event (embed x) (N.NMark k))) as H1.
+  cbn in H0,H1; rewrite He in H1; congruence.
+Qed.
+
+(* Infinite full-state room is forced by the exact ledger even if the
+   Boolean record never changes: this source of infinity is accounting. *)
+Theorem repeated_mark_states_distinct : forall k x r l n m,
+  T.run (N.nlift M K event) (repeat (N.NMark k) n) (x,r,l) =
+  T.run (N.nlift M K event) (repeat (N.NMark k) m) (x,r,l) -> n=m.
+Proof.
+  assert (Hcount : forall k n x r l,
+    snd (T.run (N.nlift M K event) (repeat (N.NMark k) n) (x,r,l))=l+n).
+  { intros k n; induction n; intros x r l; cbn; [lia|rewrite IHn; lia]. }
+  intros k x r l n m H.
+  pose proof (f_equal (@snd (T.m_state M * bool) nat) H) as E.
+  rewrite !Hcount in E; lia.
+Qed.
+End Bridge.
+
+Print Assumptions lift_as_sliced.
+Print Assumptions projection_is_retraction.
+Print Assumptions full_horizontal_embedding.
+Print Assumptions mixed_projection_erases_only_marks.
+Print Assumptions exact_ledger.
+Print Assumptions raised_record_cannot_be_decoded_from_base.
+Print Assumptions repeated_mark_states_distinct.

+(* Bridge the standalone tape model to the actual sliced interface.
+   This is a structural theorem for each transition function delta. No
+   effectivity follows for an arbitrary Coq function delta; the effective
+   universal-host theorem has its own cg_presentation hypothesis. *)
+From Coq Require Import List Arith.
+Import ListNotations.
+Require Kernel.ProperSubsumption Minimal.ThieleComplete
+  Minimal.NeutralComplete Minimal.SlicedEstablishment Audit.StructuralBridge.
+Module P := Kernel.ProperSubsumption.ProperSubsumption.
+Module T := Minimal.ThieleComplete.
+Module N := Minimal.NeutralComplete.
+Module S := Minimal.SlicedEstablishment.
+Module B := Audit.StructuralBridge.
+
+Section TapeBridge.
+Variable delta : P.TM_Delta.
+Definition tape_next (c : P.TM_Config) : P.TM_Config :=
+  match P.tm_step delta c with Some d => d | None => c end.
+Definition tape_base : T.machine :=
+  T.mk_machine P.TM_Config unit (fun c _ => tape_next c)
+    (fun _ => 0) (fun _ => false).
+Variable event : P.TM_Config -> bool.
+Definition tape_lift := N.nlift tape_base unit (fun _ => event).
+Definition tape_slice : S.SlicedInterface (T.m_state tape_lift)
+    P.TM_Config unit unit := B.lift_as_sliced tape_base unit (fun _ => event).
+
+Lemma tape_run_is_native : forall n c,
+  T.run tape_base (repeat tt n) c = P.tm_run n delta c.
+Proof.
+  induction n as [|n IH]; intro c; cbn; [reflexivity|].
+  change (T.run tape_base (repeat tt n) (tape_next c) =
+    match P.tm_step delta c with
+    | Some d => P.tm_run n delta d | None => c end).
+  unfold tape_next. destruct (P.tm_step delta c) as [d|] eqn:E.
+  - apply IH.
+  - rewrite IH. clear IH. induction n; cbn; rewrite ?E; reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem native_tape_runs_embed : forall n c,
+  T.run tape_lift (map (@N.NBase tape_base unit) (repeat tt n))
+    (B.embed tape_base c) = B.embed tape_base (P.tm_run n delta c).
+Proof.
+  intros. unfold tape_lift. rewrite B.full_horizontal_embedding,
+    tape_run_is_native. reflexivity.
+Qed.
+
+Theorem hidden_tape_record : forall c,
+  event c = true ->
+  ~ exists decode : P.TM_Config -> bool,
+      forall s : T.m_state tape_lift,
+        decode (B.base_view tape_base s) = B.record_view tape_base s.
+Proof.
+  intros c H. exact (B.raised_record_cannot_be_decoded_from_base
+    tape_base unit (fun _ => event) tt c H).
+Qed.
+End TapeBridge.
+
+Print Assumptions tape_slice.
+Print Assumptions native_tape_runs_embed.
+Print Assumptions hidden_tape_record.

+(* SCOPE NOTE: standalone proof scope.
+   Standalone finite counterexample to treating an arbitrary
+   Boolean reading as an autonomous quotient. Its toll and permanence are
+   proved directly; it is not the native earned-chain example. *)
+
+(* A Boolean observation need not be a quotient transition system. *)
+From Coq Require Import Bool Arith Lia.
+Inductive state := low | middle | high.
+Definition step (s : state) :=
+  match s with low => middle | _ => high end.
+Definition reading (s : state) :=
+  match s with high => true | _ => false end.
+Definition price := 1.
+
+Theorem toll_and_permanence :
+  (forall s, reading s = false -> reading (step s) = true -> 1 <= price) /\
+  (forall s, reading s = true -> reading (step s) = true).
+Proof. split; destruct s; cbn; intros; try discriminate; auto. Qed.
+
+Theorem no_boolean_shadow_rule :
+  ~ exists g : bool -> bool, forall s, reading (step s) = g (reading s).
+Proof.
+  intros [g H]. pose proof (H low) as H0. pose proof (H middle) as H1.
+  cbn in H0,H1; congruence.
+Qed.
+
+Print Assumptions toll_and_permanence.
+Print Assumptions no_boolean_shadow_rule.

+#!/usr/bin/env python3
+"""Reproduce the bounded checks in the root-text review.
+
+Run: python scripts/audit_root_review.py
+These finite enumerations check examples, not every theorem in the monograph.
+The publication check uses the repository's native execution semantics; only
+the external publication set is added here.
+"""
+
+from itertools import product
+from pathlib import Path
+import sys
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+from thiele_small.small import CA, exec_, fires, run, start  # noqa: E402
+
+
+def holding_graphs():
+    total = 0
+    for k in range(1, 6):
+        # Holding states 0..k-1, unfinished entrant k. All images lie in H.
+        immediate = 0
+        maximum = 0
+        for f in product(range(k), repeat=k + 1):
+            total += 1
+            immediate += f[k] in f[:k]
+            targets = {}
+            source = k
+            for collision_at in range(1, k + 2):
+                target = f[source]
+                if target in targets:
+                    assert targets[target] != source
+                    break
+                targets[target] = source
+                source = target
+            else:
+                raise AssertionError("finite holding orbit escaped collision")
+            maximum = max(maximum, collision_at - 1)
+        assert maximum == k
+        if k == 3:
+            assert immediate == 57
+        print(f"H={k}: {k ** (k + 1)} graphs; {immediate} entry collisions; "
+              f"maximum collision-free prefix {maximum}")
+    assert total == 16739
+    # An entry can collide even when the holding restriction is not onto.
+    f = (0, 0, 1, 0)
+    assert f[3] in f[:3] and set(f[:3]) != set(range(3))
+    # Finite holding can also delay the collision after entry.
+    f = (1, 2, 2, 0)
+    assert f[3] not in f[:3]
+    print("PASS: 16,739 graphs; review's entry-collision iff refuted")
+
+
+def sliced_example_and_traps():
+    for b, k in product(range(4), range(3)):
+        r = k == 2
+        rotate = ((b + 1) % 4, k)
+        advance = (b, min(k + 1, 2))
+        assert rotate[0] == (b + 1) % 4 and (rotate[1] == 2) == r
+        assert advance[0] == b and r <= (advance[1] == 2)
+        for successor, cost in ((rotate, 0), (advance, 1)):
+            assert r or successor[1] != 2 or cost >= 1
+    # A trapped representative in EVERY fibre forces h(b)=b on all B.
+    surviving = [h for h in product(range(3), repeat=3)
+                 if all(h[b] == b for b in range(3))]
+    assert surviving == [(0, 1, 2)]
+    print("PASS: 12-state sliced example; 1/27 base maps survives trapped fibres")
+
+
+def publication_fibre():
+    prepared = run([("CHECK", ("PGe", j), CA) for j in range(16)], start(16, 0))
+    assert (prepared.pc, prepared.mu, prepared.va, prepared.vb) == (17, 16, 0, 0)
+    assert prepared.facts == [(("PGe", j), CA, 0) for j in reversed(range(16))]
+    nonempty_endpoint = None
+    archives = set()
+    for mask in range(1 << 16):
+        state = prepared
+        published = set()
+        for j in range(16):
+            commit = ("COMMIT", ("PGe", j), CA)
+            state = exec_(state, commit)
+            instruction = ("CERTIFY",) if mask & (1 << j) else commit
+            if fires(state, instruction):
+                published.add(state.chan)
+            state = exec_(state, instruction)
+        expected = frozenset((("PGe", j), CA, 0)
+                             for j in range(16) if mask & (1 << j))
+        assert frozenset(published) == expected
+        assert (state.pc, state.mu, state.chan, state.err) == (
+            49, 48, (("PGe", 15), CA, 0), False)
+        assert state.cert == bool(mask)
+        if mask:
+            if nonempty_endpoint is None:
+                nonempty_endpoint = state.key()
+            assert state.key() == nonempty_endpoint
+            archives.add(expected)
+        else:
+            empty_endpoint = state.copy()
+    assert len(archives) == 65535
+    empty_endpoint.cert = True
+    assert empty_endpoint.key() == nonempty_endpoint
+    print("PASS: 65,536 native runs; 65,535 nonempty archives above one full state")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    holding_graphs()
+    sliced_example_and_traps()
+    publication_fibre()
